> 🇧🇷 Versão em Português — <a href="/rpg-mo-wiki/breeding">see the English page</a>

Breeding is a [skill](/skills) that allows the player to obtain new [pets](/pets) by using 2 breeding [Nests](https://rpg-mo.fandom.com/wiki/Nest) inside a [Fence](https://rpg-mo.fandom.com/wiki/Fence) and 2 parent pets. Each breeding attempt can only produce 1 new pet, if successful. The offspring will be a different type than the parents.

The breeding might fail and, on top of that, there is 50% chance of one of the parents, the "mother", will die. Insuring a pet before a breeding attempt prevents its death, but there's no way to know if the pet insured will be considered the mother, so to be sure the mother won't die, both pets should be insured unless it would be less expensive to replace the mother.

To learn how to start breeding, see: [Beginners guide to breeding](https://rpg-mo.fandom.com/wiki/Beginners_guide_to_breeding).

A second breeding resource can be found [HERE](https://modb.rpgmobob.com/#/breeding).

Click a [Nest](https://rpg-mo.fandom.com/wiki/Nest) inside a breeding pen and click Formula for even more breeding information.

Note that reduced [Captcha](/captcha) rates apply to breeding. To learn how to properly calculate the actual success chances for each offspring, read: [Success chance](/success-chance)

## Feeding Formula

((HA - HU) / HU) x 100 = Z. Z / F =Total food % needed rounded up. So, HAppiness minutes minus HUnger minutes divided by HUnger minutes times 100 tells you how many percents of total food you need for a breeding. As an example, Nightmare Ghost takes 16 minutes to be happy and 6 minutes of hunger before dying. 16 - 6 = 10 minutes of food needed. 10 / 6 = 1.666 x 100 = 166.6% total food needed. Rounding up gives you 167%. The foods available are: Undead Eye - 15%, Dust - 20%, Uncut High Grade Crystal - 30% and Cut Crystal - 40%. 5 Uncut High Grade Crystals = 150% + 1 Dust = 170% total. Only feed a single food unit when Hunger % is higher than food %. Mousing over each food shows the percents of each and mousing over the Happiness and Hunger bars tells you the minutes of each.

## Breeding Foods

As this page is very long I have decided to link a separate list of [Breeding Foods](/breeding-foods).

## Breeding Table

Note: Since the first offspring must fail for the second to be possible, the adjusted chance for second offspring is half of the value shown. Those breedings with three or more offspring will also be much lower than shown.

Breeding order is now: Rare -> Legendary ->Ancient -> Rare Egg -> Legendary Egg -> Ancient Egg -> Common -> Common Egg.
